You’ve collected 25 cards. 25 is\nPikachu’s National Pokédex\nnumber! When several Pikachu\ngather, their electricity can build\nand cause lightning storms.
You’ve collected 50 cards. 50 is\nDiglett’s National Pokédex number!\nIt lives about one yard underground,\nwhere it feeds on plant roots. It\nsometimes appears aboveground.
The number 80 brings to mind\nTentacruel! Its 80 tentacles can\nstretch and shrink freely. Tentacruel\nensnares prey in a net of spread-out\ntentacles, delivering venomous stings\nto its catch.
The number 100 brings to mind\nMachop! Its whole body is composed\nof muscles. Even though it’s the size\nof a human child, it can hurl 100\ngrown-ups.
The number 151 brings to mind the\nPokédex! A total of 151 Pokémon\nwere introduced in [Ctrl:Italic ]Pokémon Red[/Ctrl:Italic]\nand [Ctrl:Italic ]Pokémon Blue[/Ctrl:Italic].
The number 180 brings to mind\nNoctowl! When it needs to think, it\nrotates its head 180 degrees to\nsharpen its intellectual power.
You’ve collected 250 cards. 250 is\nHo-Oh’s National Pokédex\nnumber! It will reveal itself before\na pure-hearted Trainer by shining\nits bright, rainbow-colored wings.
The number 300 brings to mind\nFlorges! It is said that there was once\na Florges that protected the garden of\na castle for over 300 years.
The number 500 brings to mind\nSpiritomb! It was bound to a fissure in\nan odd keystone as punishment for\nmisdeeds 500 years ago.
You’ve collected 777 cards. 777 is\nTogedemaru’s National Pokédex\nnumber! When it’s in trouble, it\ncurls up into a ball, makes its fur\nspikes stand on end, and then\ndischarges electricity indiscriminately.
The number 1,000 brings to mind\nJirachi! It is said to have the ability to\ngrant any wish for just one week\nevery thousand years.
The number 1,500 brings to mind\nGimmighoul! This Pokémon was born\ninside a treasure chest about 1,500\nyears ago. It sucks the life-force out of\nscoundrels who try to steal the\ntreasure.
The number 2,000 brings to mind\nConkeldurr! It is thought that\nConkeldurr taught humans how to\nmake concrete more than 2,000\nyears ago.
The number 5,000 brings to mind\nAlakazam! It is said to have an IQ of\napproximately 5,000. Its overflowing\npsychokinetic powers cause headaches\nto anyone nearby.
The number 10,000 brings to mind\nWartortle! It is said to live 10,000\nyears. Its furry tail is popular as a\nsymbol of longevity.
The number 15,000 brings to mind\nToxtricity! The jolts of electricity it\nlaunches by violently strumming the\nprotrusions on its chest easily exceed\n15,000 volts.
The number 20,000 brings to mind\nClaydol! This mysterious Pokémon\nstarted life as an ancient clay figurine\nmade over 20,000 years ago.
The number 100,000 brings to mind\nRaichu! It can loose 100,000-volt\nbursts of electricity, instantly downing\nfoes several times its size.

Victory!
Take a look at the Greninja in the \nActive Spot.
Abilities are special powers that Pokémon\nhave that are different from attacks.
Unlike attacks, using an Ability does not\nend your turn.
There are Abilities that work all the time.
This Greninja has the \nWater Shuriken Ability.
This powerful Ability lets you do damage \nin addition to its attack damage.
Try using Greninja’s Ability.
You’ve attached an Energy, so use the\nattack to Knock Out Jynx.
Use an Ability!
Knock Out your opponent’s Pokémon to\nearn points.
If you’re the first to reach 3 points,\nyou win the battle.
Points will be displayed here.
Use Pikachu’s Gnaw attack to Knock Out\nyour opponent’s Eevee.
Use an attack!
Start your turn by drawing a card from\nyour deck.
You can play cards that are in your hand\nduring your turn.
Play Pikachu from your hand to\nyour Bench.
If you have no Pokémon left in play, you\nlose the battle.
But now, even if your Active Pokémon is\nKnocked Out, you don’t need to worry!
Eevee in the Active Spot was\nKnocked Out.
Choose a Benched Pokémon to move to\nthe Active Spot.
Play Pokémon from\nyour hand!

OK
Back
Pokémon need Energy to use attacks.
This is the Energy Zone.
When it is your turn, an Energy is\ngenerated in the Energy Zone.
You can also see the Energy that will be\ngenerated during your next turn.
Attach an Energy to Pikachu.
Pikachu now has one Lightning Energy.
It can now use its attack, Gnaw.
Attach Energy!
Energy can be attached to a\nBenched Pokémon, too.
By attaching Energy to a Benched Pokémon\nyou will be prepared to attack.
Pikachu already has one Lightning Energy,\nso it can use its attack.
Attach a Lightning Energy to the Raichu on\nyour Bench.
You attached an Energy to Raichu on your\nlast turn, so it can almost use its attack.
Attach one more Energy to the Raichu and\nuse its attack.
Attach Energy to your\nBenched Pokémon!
Let’s try evolving a Pokémon during battle!
Take a good look at the cards in your hand.
Here, it says “Evolves from Charmeleon.”
You can evolve the Pokémon that is shown\nhere with the correct Evolution Pokémon.
Charmeleon is the Pokémon in the\nActive Spot.
Evolve Pokémon in play by putting Evolution\nPokémon from your hand on top of them.
Pokémon can have higher HP and stronger\nattacks after evolving.
Even if a Pokémon evolves, it keeps all\nattached Energy and any damage.
You can evolve as many Pokémon as you\nwant on your turn, except on your\nfirst turn.
You can’t evolve a Pokémon on its first\nturn in play or if it already evolved during\nthis turn.
Evolve your Pokémon!
There are other types of cards that are\nuseful in battle besides Pokémon cards.
Take a look at the cards in your hand.
This is a Trainer card.
Trainer cards are divided into Items and\nSupporters depending on usage and effect.
This is the Item card, Potion.
Playing this card will heal one of your\nPokémon’s HP.
Pikachu has 10 HP remaining.
Play Potion to heal Pikachu’s HP.
You may play as many Item cards as you\nlike during your turn.
Since Pikachu’s HP was healed, it\ndidn’t get Knocked Out.
Play an Item card!
Cards aren’t only for collecting. They can\nalso be used in battle.
Take a look at the Pikachu card.
Pokémon have HP. When they lose all of\ntheir HP, they are Knocked Out.
Here you can find a card’s attacks. With\nthese, you can attack your opponent’s\nPokémon and use the attacks’ effects.
You and your opponent’s Pokémon battle\none another as you try to Knock Out all\nof their Pokémon one by one.
In a battle, you can use cards you’ve\ncollected to face off against your opponent.
In this guide, you can battle with cards\nthat have been set up for you.
The cards near you are your in-play zone,\nand the cards on the other side are your\nopponent’s in-play zone.
This is your Active Spot, and it’s where\nyour Pokémon battle using attacks.
Pokémon in the Active Spot are referred\nto as Active Pokémon.
This is your Bench, and it’s where you put\nPokémon that can switch with your Active\nPokémon.
Pokémon on the Bench are referred to as\nBenched Pokémon.
This is your deck.
Your cards are placed here face down, and\nyou draw one on your turn.
This is your hand.
You can have up to 10 cards in your hand,\nand you use these cards during the battle.
Starting in the next part of the guide, we’ll\ngo through the steps of a battle.
Let’s start battling!
Tap and hold
You have one Trainer card in your hand.
Let’s take a good look at it.
This is the Pokémon Tool card,\nGiant Cape.
You can attach Pokémon Tools to your Active\nPokémon or to your Benched Pokémon.
Once a Pokémon Tool has been attached to\na Pokémon, it remains attached until the\nPokémon leaves play.
You can use as many Pokémon Tools during\nyour turn as you’d like, but each Pokémon can\nhave only one attached at a time.
This card gives +20 HP to the Pokémon it’s\nattached to.
If your Pokémon is attacked on your\nopponent’s next turn, it will be Knocked Out.
Let’s try attaching Giant Cape.
Your Pokémon’s remaining HP is now 70, and\nit looks like it’ll be able to withstand\none attack.
Let’s attach an Energy and end your turn.
Because its HP was increased, Raichu wasn’t\nKnocked Out.
Play a Pokémon Tool card!
Before starting the battle, let’s set up\nyour Pokémon.
First, put a Pokémon from your hand into\nthe Active Spot.
Take a good look at the cards in your hand.
You can play any Pokémon that says Basic.
Place a Basic Pokémon in the Active Spot.
Once you’re done setting up your Active\nPokémon, next is setting up your Bench.
During a real battle, you have the option to\nset up your Bench.
For this guide, let’s practice setting it up.
You can put a Basic Pokémon here,\njust like in the Active Spot.
Once you’ve set up your Pokémon,\ngo ahead and tap Start Battle.
Now, you’re ready to begin the battle.
Prepare for battle!
You can switch your Active Pokémon with\nyour Benched Pokémon.
This is called retreating.
Take a look at Pikachu’s Retreat Cost.
The Retreat Cost is one Energy.
Let’s retreat Pikachu and switch it \nwith Raichu.
If you retreat, the Energy cost will be\ndiscarded from that Pokémon.
You can retreat once per turn.
You can still use an attack even if you\nretreated during that turn.
Retreat!
Take a look at Grimer in the Active Spot.
It says that your opponent’s Active\nPokémon is now Poisoned.
Attacks can have all kinds of effects like this\napart from just damage.
This attack can make your opponent’s\nPokémon Poisoned.
Use Poison Gas to make your opponent’s\nPokémon Poisoned.
You made your opponent’s Caterpie\nPoisoned.
Poisoned Pokémon take 10 damage during\nPokémon Checkup.
Pokémon will recover from Special\nConditions, such as Poisoned, when they\nmove to the Bench.
Apart from Poisoned, there are other\nSpecial Conditions such as Paralyzed and\nAsleep.
For more information, check out Tips.
Apply Special Conditions!
You have one Trainer card in your hand.
Let’s take a good look at it.
This is called a Stadium card.
When a Stadium card is in play, it has an effect\non both you and your opponent.
You can play only 1 Stadium card from your\nhand during your turn, and it stays in play.
Only 1 Stadium card can be in play at a time.
When a Stadium with a different name is put\nin play, discard the Stadium that was\npreviously in play and replace it.
You can’t play a Stadium card with the \nsame name as the Stadium already in play.
This card makes attacks used by both\nplayers’ Stage 1 Pokémon do +10 damage\nto their opponent’s Active Pokémon.
Let’s take a look at this Charmeleon card.
Because Charmeleon is a Stage 1 Pokémon,\nwe can apply the effect of Training Area and\nincrease the damage done by its attacks.
Put Training Area into play.
We discarded the Stadium that your opponent\nput in play and replaced it with Training Area.
Now, Charmeleon’s attack can Knock Out\nWartortle.
Play a Stadium card!
You only have one Trainer card in\nyour hand.
Let’s take a good look at it.
This is the Supporter card,\nProfessor’s Research.
Unlike Item cards, you can only use one\nSupporter card on your turn.
If you play Professor’s Research, you can\ndraw two cards from your deck.
At this rate, Pikachu will get Knocked Out.
Play Professor’s Research and draw cards\nfrom your deck.
It looks like you drew a Raichu.
Let’s evolve Pikachu into Raichu and use\nan attack.
Play a Supporter card!

The opponent’s Squirtle is Water-type.

Squirtle’s Weakness is Lightning-type.
Let’s use an attack by your\nLightning-type Pikachu.

Promo Packs
PROMO
One of the following cards, chosen randomly, will be included in the promo packs you can obtain from this event.
About promo packs
In drop events, you can battle all sorts of decks,\nand you might obtain promo packs if you win.\nYou can obtain special cards from these promo\npacks.\nNote: The promo packs and cards in the image\nare an example.
You will need event stamina in order to\nparticipate in event battles.\nEvent stamina can be recovered over time and by\nusing items called event hourglasses.
Emblem events are events where you can battle\nagainst other players and strive for emblems of\nhigher ranks by winning as many battles as you\ncan. The emblems you get can be used on your\nplayer profile.
There are several kinds of emblem events, such as\nthose where you aim to get a certain number of\nvictories, aim for consecutive victories, and more.\nYou can check what kind of event is underway by\nchecking the announcements for the event.
In drop events, you can battle all sorts of decks,\nand you might obtain promo packs if you win.\nYou can obtain special cards from these promo\npacks.\nNote: The promo packs and cards in the image\nare an example.
You will need event stamina in order to\nparticipate in event battles.\nEvent stamina can be recovered over time and by\nusing items called event hourglasses.

Welcome to\nPokémon Trading Card Game Pocket!
In this game, you can enjoy building a Pokémon\nTrading Card Game collection on your mobile\ndevice.\n\nTo begin, please tell us your player name.
This is the end of the basic tutorial.\n\nNow try exploring some of the game’s features!
Pack stamina refills, so you can open booster\npacks every day.
Try unlocking new features by completing\nbeginner missions, getting player EXP, and\nincreasing your level.

In Wonder Pick, you can get one of the cards\n(chosen randomly) from a booster pack that was\nopened by another player.\n\nNote: That player will not lose any cards.
Wonder picks are updated at regular intervals.\nBooster packs opened by your friends may also\nappear as wonder picks.\n\nTry to check in regularly to see what’s new.
Wonder picking requires wonder stamina, which\nrecovers over time.\n\nYou can reduce the time it takes until wonder\nstamina recovers by using items.
There are also special kinds of wonder picks that\nmay appear, like bonus picks and Chansey picks.
An expired wonder pick can be made available\nagain by using a rewind watch.
Let’s take a look at the cards you’ve registered\nin the card dex.
Tap on the mission you completed to\nclaim a reward.
You can open a booster pack once your\npack stamina is full.

Notifications can let you know when you’re able\nto open packs.\n\nYou can turn notifications on by going to the\nmenu, Other, and then Settings.

Your player name cannot be changed again for\n30 days.

Pack stamina refills, so you can use it to open\nbooster packs every day.\n\nPack stamina recovery time can be reduced by\nusing pack hourglasses or Poké gold.
You can tap on the button on the bottom-right\npart of the screen if you want to open a different\nbooster pack.\n\nYou can then choose a booster pack from among\nthose that have become available so far.
You will be notified when your pack stamina has\nrecovered and you can open a pack.\n\nAnd remember, you can open your choice of\nbooster packs every day.
You can tap a card to enlarge it and view its\ninformation in detail.
You can also see cards you do not own if you tap\nthe card dex toggle switch.
If you obtain duplicates of a card, you can obtain\nflair which can be used to change that card’s\nappearance.\n\nPlease see the videos for further details.
You can tap this icon if you want to see this\nexplanation again.\n\nIf you’re unsure of anything else, you can try\nchecking Tips.
In Binders, you can create binders with cards you\nhave collected.\n\nThere are a variety of covers that can be obtained\nin a number of ways, like from the Shop or by\ncompleting missions.
You can share the binders you’ve created with\nfriends and other players.\n\nYou can view what other players have shared in\nSocial Hub.
Try creating binders that are uniquely yours!
You can tap this icon if you want to see this\nexplanation again.\n\nIf you’re unsure of anything else, you can try\nchecking Tips.
In Display Boards, you can place cards on\nbackdrops and show them off.\n\nThere are a variety of backdrops that can be\nobtained in a number of ways, like from the Shop\nor by completing missions.
You can share the display boards you’ve created\nwith friends or other players.\n\nYou can view what other players have shared in\nSocial Hub.
Try creating display boards that are\nuniquely yours!
You can tap this icon if you want to see this\nexplanation again.\n\nIf you’re unsure of anything else, you can try\nchecking Tips.
You will receive a deck once you complete part 06\nof the battle guide, “Evolve your Pokémon!”\n\nPlay your way through the battle guide\nto receive other rewards, like pack hourglasses.
In My Decks, you can build decks to use in battle.
A deck is made up of a set of 20 cards.
You can also build a deck using Auto-build.\n\nAll you have to do is select Auto-build, then one\nor more types, and a deck will be automatically\nbuilt using cards you own.
You can also change your playmat, card sleeves,\nand Pokémon coin.\n\nThese can be obtained in a number of ways, like\nfrom the Shop or by completing missions.
You can tap this icon if you want to see this\nexplanation again.\n\nIf you’re unsure of anything else, you can try\nchecking Tips.
Rental decks are a kind of deck that let you\nborrow a set of 20 cards to use in battle.
Rental decks are obtained mainly by completing\ndeck missions.\n\nYou can see the rental decks you have obtained\nfrom the Deck Menu.
A rental deck has a limited number of uses.
You can use theme deck lists to easily build decks.
If you select a theme deck list when building a\ndeck, a deck will be built using cards you own.\n\nTo build a theme deck, you must own all the cards\nrequired by the theme deck list.
There are all sorts of missions available.\n\nYou can try to complete as many as you can to\nearn rewards.
For missions that involve collecting specific cards,\nyou will be able to see additional details about the\nones you need to collect.\n\nYou can try to collect them by opening packs and\nwonder picking.
You accumulate pack points by opening booster\npacks. Pack points can be used to get cards of\nyour choosing.
You accumulate pack points by using pack stamina\nto open booster packs.\n\nYou can exchange a set amount of pack points for\ncards of your choosing.
Opening booster packs of a given expansion earns\nyou pack points for that expansion.\n\nThose points can be exchanged only for cards that\nappear in that expansion.
An expansion is a group of similarly labeled\nbooster packs.\n\nYou can view different expansions by tapping\nSelect other booster packs at the bottom-right\npart of the packs screen.
As you collect certain cards, you will be able to\nobtain rental decks which let you try a deck in\nbattle.\n\nThere is a limit to how many times you can use a\nparticular rental deck.
Once you collect the 20 cards listed in a deck\nmission for a given deck, you will obtain its theme\ndeck list.\n\nYou can then build that same deck anytime by\ntapping Build New from the Decks screen.
If you run out of pack stamina, you can’t open any\npacks until your stamina has recovered.\n\nLet’s try reducing recovery time with pack\nhourglasses.
You can get pack hourglasses in a variety of ways,\nlike when increasing your player level or as\nmission rewards, and you can use them as soon as\nyou get them!
When you subscribe to the premium pass, you can\nopen one more pack every day.\n\nFirst-time subscribers to the premium pass may\nuse it at no cost for two weeks. You can subscribe\nfrom the Shop.
First, choose one friend to trade with and send a\ntrade offer.
Then, choose just one card to send to that friend,\nand offer it to trade. After you send your trade\noffer, wait for their response.
When you get a response, a notification will\nappear on the Trade screen. View the card\nthey’re offering and tap OK.
Once a trade agreement is reached, you and your\ntrade partner’s cards will be exchanged.
In order to trade, you need trade stamina.\nTrade stamina recovers over time. You can\nuse items to reduce the time it takes to recover.
Trades can be done with cards of the same rarity.\nTo trade high-rarity cards, you need shinedust.
Shinedust can be obtained when you acquire\ncards already registered in your card dex, as \nmission rewards, and so forth.
You can now share which cards are on your\nwishlist when trading with other players.
You can wishlist cards from the card details screen\nin My Cards.
Up to three wishlisted cards can be set to display\non your profile and on your friends list when\ntrading.\nYou can set these cards on the profile screen.
You can check the card that your trade partner is\nplanning to send you. If you want the card, you can\nproceed to the next step. You can also decline the\noffer.
To receive that card from your trade partner, you\nchoose just one card to send to them in response.\nOnce you’ve responded, wait for your trade\npartner’s OK.
When your trade partner accepts the trade, a\nnotification will appear on the Trade screen. Once\nyou get the notification you can receive their card.
In Ranked Matches, there is a rank system that\ngoes from Beginner Rank to Master Ball Rank.\nYou can go up in rank based on the points you\nget when you win battles.
In Master Ball Rank, the top players will have their\nranking displayed.
The ranked match season will change about once\na month. After a season ends, players can get\nemblems based on their rank at the time the\nseason ended.
Choose a card you will send to the other person.\n\nYou will receive from them a card of the same\nrarity as the card you chose.

Choose a card to send to the other person.\n\nOnly cards of the same rarity can be traded.

Select an item or flair to see which cards you can\nexchange for it.

It’s up to you to decide what themes to use.\nYou can create display boards however you’d like.

You can try building decks with your own\ncards to participate in battles.

You can try using it once you have\ncollected a lot of cards.

Booster Pack Selection Probability
Attention
The offering rate for each of the following cards is that card’s offering rate when a booster pack in this category is pulled.

[Text:CardName id="0" ] [C:Lsb ]Parallel foil card[C:Rsb ]
When a pack is opened, the steps listed below will be carried out in sequence. (Further details follow.)\n\nStep 1: Pack Selection\nStep 2: Card Selection\n\nAbout step 1: Pack Selection\nPack selection is the first selection process that is carried out.\nIn pack selection, a pack to be opened will be selected so that step 2, card selection, can occur.\nPlease see the Offering Rates tab to confirm the category names, offering rates, and number of cards in the packs that are generated.\n\nAbout step 2: Card Selection\nAfter the pack to be opened is decided in step 1, pack selection, step 2, card selection, is carried out.\nIn card selection, the cards that are actually generated will be selected.\nThe categories of cards offered and the offering rates differ for each pack. The number of cards in the packs may also differ. For example, if a “regular” pack is selected, cards will be generated as displayed in the categories, offering rates, and number of cards for “regular packs.” If a “rare” pack is selected, cards will be generated as displayed in the categories, offering rates, and number of cards for “rare packs.”\n\n• Cards from packs not selected in pack selection will not be generated. For example, if a “regular” pack is selected, cards will be generated from “regular” packs. (Cards from “rare” packs will not be generated.)\n• Please see the Offering Rates tab to confirm the specific probability rates for card selection.\n• The offering rate displayed for each card is the offering rate after the pack category is decided in pack selection.\n• The cards in a pack are selected one at a time.\n• Booster packs that are available for a limited time only may be offered again after the availability period has ended.\n• Cards available in this pack may also be available in packs added at a future date.\n• The availability period and details are subject to change without warning.\n\nAdditional notes:\n• It’s possible for the offering rate of cards included in the same pack to be different.\n• It’s possible for the order of the cards in the results to differ from the order that the cards were actually selected in.\n• It’s possible for the same card to be selected and offered two or more times in the same pack as duplicates.\n• The offering rates are cut off after the third decimal place and may not equal 100% when added together.\n• Actual offering rates have not been rounded up or down.\n• Changes may be made, such as adjustments to card details or bans on certain cards from being used, as a result of error fixes or adjustments made to maintain a fair battle environment.\n• The language of the cards will be the app’s selected language at the time a pack is opened.\n• The language of cards already obtained will stay the same as it was at the time they were obtained and will not be affected if the app’s selected language is changed after they were obtained.\n\nAbout “[Mst:Rarity v="RARITY_MSID_RR" ] or higher guaranteed” packs\n• If the condition below is fulfilled, the next time a pack from the same expansion as the pack for which the condition has been fulfilled is opened, a [Mst:Rarity v="RARITY_MSID_RR" ] or higher card will be generated.\n\nCondition: A [Mst:Rarity v="RARITY_MSID_RR" ] or higher card did not get generated after 12 consecutive openings of packs from the same expansion.\nNote: Information related to fulfilling the condition, such as the current number of consecutive openings, or the number of openings remaining to fulfill the condition, will not be displayed.\n\n• If the condition is fulfilled, the pack categories selected during step 1, pack selection, will change.\n• During pack selection, the “[Mst:Rarity v="RARITY_MSID_RR" ] or higher guaranteed” pack category will be added but will not be the only category offered. It is possible for “rare” or “regular +1 card” pack categories to be offered instead.\nNote: In that case, a [Mst:Rarity v="RARITY_MSID_RR" ] or higher card is still guaranteed to be generated.\n• For more details, please see the “Offering Rates” tab.
• A “regular” pack will not be selected if the conditions for the booster pack in this category to be generated have been fulfilled.\n• In place of it, the booster pack in this category will be selected.\n• The selection probability will be the same as the offering rates of a “regular” pack.\n• Please check the Attention section for information on pack generation conditions.
• A “regular” or “regular + 1 card” pack will not be selected if the conditions for the booster pack in this category to be generated have been fulfilled.\n• In place of it, the booster pack in this category will be selected.\n• The selection probability will be that of a “regular” and a “regular + 1 card” pack combined.\n• Please check the Attention section for information on pack generation conditions.

Premium Pass
[Ctrl:Bold ]■ About the contract\n• When you subscribe to the premium pass, your subscription will be automatically renewed every month unless you cancel at least 24 hours before the premium pass expiration date.[/Ctrl:Bold]\n• For more information on premium pass cancellation methods, reimbursement options, payment periods and methods, transferring data, and changing devices, please refer to Customer Support.\n• If you cancel your subscription, the monthly cost will not be prorated.\n• If you choose to cancel your plan before it has reached its renewal date, you will still have premium pass benefits until the expiration date.\n• Digital content such as in-game currencies and in-game items are offered as in-game licensed materials.\n\n■ About the no-cost trial\n• The first time you purchase a premium pass, you won’t be charged for the first 14 days.\n• After completing the no-cost trial, your plan will automatically be converted to a paid premium pass plan that renews automatically.\n• If you would like to only subscribe to the premium pass while it is available at no cost, please cancel your subscription at least 24 hours before the end of the no-cost trial. For information on cancellation methods, please refer to Customer Support.\n• If you repeatedly make inappropriate use of the no-cost trial with methods other than those intended by our company, we may suspend your account or take other actions.\n<color=#F23E44>• In some cases, the one-time two-week no-cost trial may not be applied due to app store specifications.\n• Please confirm that the no-cost trial is displayed on the app store payment screen before subscribing.</color>\n• If the App[C:Nbsp ]Store or Google[C:Nbsp ]Play deems that a no-cost trial is not applicable, even if it is your first subscription, the no-cost trial may not be applied.\n• Please check the App[C:Nbsp ]Store or Google[C:Nbsp ]Play purchase screen to confirm whether or not the no-cost trial is applicable. If the no-cost trial is not displayed on the purchase screen, please note that the no-cost trial will not be applied and a fee will be charged at the time of subscription.\n\n■ About benefits\nBenefit 1: You can open one more pack every day\n• A new pack stamina gauge will be added. That gauge will let you open one additional pack every 24 hours.\n• The maximum storage that pack stamina added by the premium pass can reach is two packs worth.\n• Premium pass pack stamina cannot be recovered using items such as pack hourglasses.\n\nBenefit 2: You can take on premium missions that let you get promo cards\n• You’ll be able to take on premium missions, and if you complete those missions, you can get premium tickets and other rewards.\n• If you collect premium tickets, you can exchange set amounts of them for various items.\n• Premium missions and the items that can be exchanged for using premium tickets are regularly refreshed/changed. After a refresh, the items cannot be claimed.\n• You can refer to the following screens for information about items or their availability periods:\nThe Premium tab on the Missions screen\nThe Premium tab on the Shop screen\n• Cards received will be cards of the app’s selected language.\n• If you cancel a subscription and then subscribe again, you will not be able to obtain items you already claimed.
■To iOS users:\n• If your AppIe Account has already been used for a trial, you will no longer be eligible for a 14-day no-cost trial period.\n• To confirm the expiration date of your pass, open the Settings app on your device, tap on your Apple Account, then tap Subscriptions.\nNote: These options may appear differently on your device, depending on your OS version.
■ To Android users:\n• If your Google Account has already been used for a trial, you will no longer be eligible for a 14-day no-cost trial period.\n• To confirm the expiration date of your pass, open the Play Store, tap on your profile icon, then select Payments & Subscriptions. You should find the information under Subscriptions.

Trade Rules
It’s only possible to trade cards of the same rarity.
If you apply flair, the card you receive from your trade partner must also have flair applied.
The following cards cannot be traded:
• Certain high-rarity cards\n• Cards from certain expansions\n• Promo cards\n\nNote: This will be periodically updated. For more details, please see “Detailed trade list.”
You need shinedust to trade cards of high rarity.

If you delete your save data, you will lose all\nyour data—including your record of play to\nthis point, the items you own, and your Poké gold.\n\nEven if you linked your save data to an account,\nthe data will be completely deleted and you will no\nlonger be able to play using it. Even if you link\nagain to an account, you will not be able to\ncontinue playing with the save data, and you will\nnot be able to restore it.\n\nNotes: \n-All unused Poké gold (paid) will be\ndeleted, and no refunds can be received once\nservice has been ended.\n-For premium pass subscribers, your premium\npass subscription will not be automatically\ncanceled, even if you delete your save data.\nPlease be sure to cancel your subscription from\nthe store where you purchased it.\n\nDo you want to delete your save data?
